Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the plasma_physics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 716. The resulting operator achieves bound O(187/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to plasma_physics theory, we decompose the problem into 716 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 187. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the existence and uniqueness fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
